# Agents · L · part 11

970 entries, log-leak-reviewer to loop-planner. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **log-leak-reviewer**: Audit a diff for sensitive data escaping BrowserMesh - secrets, tokens, saved browser state, page contents, form values, or filesystem paths reaching logs, MCP results, or error messages. Use before merging any change that touches logging, error mapping, MCP results, persistence, diagnostics, or the CLI. ([scrollDynasty/BrowserMesh](https://github.com/scrollDynasty/BrowserMesh/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/log-leak-reviewer.md) · ★3)
- **log-monitor**: Monitor BG3SE log file during testing sessions. Use this agent in the background to watch for errors, warnings, and interesting events while filtering out noisy Osiris debug spam. ([tdimino/bg3se-macos](https://github.com/tdimino/bg3se-macos/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/log-monitor.md) · ★66)
- **log-monitor**: **Role**: Log analysis & pattern detection **Mode**: Subagent ([0xRayAI/xray](https://github.com/0xRayAI/xray/blob/HEAD/docs-site/docs/archive/agents/log-monitor.md) · ★5 · 2 copies)
- **log-monitor**: DEPRECATED: Superseded by atm-monitor. Monitors ATM/Codex logging surfaces, answers system status questions from logs, tails with filters until matching events occur, and notifies teammates on warn/error conditions. ([randlee/agent-team-mail](https://github.com/randlee/agent-team-mail/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/log-monitor.md) · ★2)
- **log-monitoring-expert**: Expert on THIS repo's logging, log-based alerting, and observability. Use for anything touching how the bot captures/forwards logs and turns them into Lark alerts: the root-logger handler (p0_logic/monitoring_notify.py LarkMonitoringLogHandler), P0_MONITORING_* toggles, log levels (ERROR vs WARNING) and why an event did/didn't alert, dedupe/cooldown, the P0 session log summary + real-time error-to-group flow, journalctl on the VPS (unit is `lark-ops-ai`, NOT lark-ops-ai-dev), and adding/tuning alerts. Reach for it when asked "why didn't this alert", "summarize the logs", or to design new log-… ([Renzel025/lark-ops-ai](https://github.com/Renzel025/lark-ops-ai/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/log-monitoring-expert.md))
- **log-operator**: Operatore dei log applicativi (log_access, opt-in). Esegue indagini in più passi sui log OpenSearch degli ambienti e delle app del registro via tools/logs/log_access.py con le credenziali del file env configurato per target. Sola lettura (solo _search, _count, _field_caps), dati personali mascherati di default; analizza ticket ed errori su una feature e restituisce un blocco JSON con evidenze citate. Gated su log_access.enabled. ([Almusweb/wiki-factory](https://github.com/Almusweb/wiki-factory/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/log-operator.md))
- **log-ops-retention**: Ciclo de vida do log EM DISCO: pasta por dia UTC, rotação por tamanho, um arquivo por processo, streams separados por finalidade, retenção escalonada com guardas de exclusão, guarda de disco cheio, permissões e volume persistente. Herda envelope/correlação de observability e redação de runtime-secrets — não redefine nenhum dos dois. ([pretinhuu1-boop/blindar](https://github.com/pretinhuu1-boop/blindar/blob/HEAD/agents/log-ops-retention.md))
- **log-optimizer**: Analyzes test-run result logs after a run and reports concrete improvements — faster runs, fewer errors, more parallelism, higher coverage, better teardown ordering. Spawn it (ideally in the background) at the end of EVERY test run, and use it periodically to read several days of logs for trends/patterns. Use when the user asks to "analyze the run", "find improvements", "why was it slow", "tune parallelism / deletion order", or after smoke/CRUD/heavy runs finish. ([kyle-agent/api-test-automation](https://github.com/kyle-agent/api-test-automation/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/log-optimizer.md) · ★1)
- **log-parser**: Read-only log parsing agent for the nginx-hardening plugin. Extracts structured data from nginx access logs, hex-encodes all attacker-controlled fields. Never proposes actions or writes files. Use when the analyze-logs command needs to parse raw nginx logs into structured format for the sanitization pipeline. ([trumb/claude-nginx-hardening](https://github.com/trumb/claude-nginx-hardening/blob/HEAD/agents/log-parser.md) · ★4)
- **log-parser**: Extracts relevant stack trace/log lines from raw Databricks run output. ([felipecastro-data/self-healing-databricks-pipeline-multi-agent](https://github.com/felipecastro-data/self-healing-databricks-pipeline-multi-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/log-parser.md))

- **log-reviewer**: Audit roku-log usage in a JellyRock BS/BRS file or function. Defaults to audit only: lists logging gaps without proposing additions. When explicitly asked to propose fixes, proposes only critical-error-path additions (silent error paths, untracked state changes that would make a class of bug invisible). Never blanket-adds entry logs, variable-trace logs or function-trace logging; it refuses that. Use when you want a quick read on whether logging in a file is adequate, or when another agent needs a focused logging audit before proposing a fix. ([jellyrock/jellyrock](https://github.com/jellyrock/jellyrock/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/log-reviewer.md) · ★53)

- **logic-gate-postop**: Cross-family POST-OP reasoning gate. A second, sightless verdict on finished work. You are handed the original pre-op packet, the verdict that packet earned, and the resulting diff — but never the driver's own assessment of what it built. - **loop**: Have your agent edit a page, check it, fix what's reported, and ask the commands that answer what it would otherwise guess. ([ascribed-dev/ascribe](https://github.com/ascribed-dev/ascribe/blob/HEAD/docs/content/agents/loop.md))
- **loop**: **Cross-link**: [.claude/commands/loop.md](../../.claude/commands/loop.md) · 근거: ADR-036 ([bbabi0901/portfolio](https://github.com/bbabi0901/portfolio/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/loop.md))
- **loop**: loop ([blennster/dotfiles](https://github.com/blennster/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/shell/dot-config/opencode/agents/loop.md))
- **loop**: loop-manager is developed by a loop of coder → reviewer under a master (`overseer`), with no operator. The protocol of a cycle is in the agent definitions (`.claude/agents/`) and in `scripts/loop/loopctl`; this page holds what is common to the roles. ([kvokka/loop-manager](https://github.com/kvokka/loop-manager/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/loop.md))
- **loop**: practice: spec: to-spec ([kzarzycki/agent-skills](https://github.com/kzarzycki/agent-skills/blob/HEAD/engineering/tests/fixtures/loop-project/docs/agents/loop.md))
- **loop**: The engineering loop's values for this repo. `engineering/skills/engineering-loop/SKILL.md` (Approvals and proof) defines what each setting approves and what still waits for the owner. ([kzarzycki/agent-skills](https://github.com/kzarzycki/agent-skills/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/loop.md))
- **loop**: The `engineering-loop` skill reads this file at every step but the gate. ([kzarzycki/dotagents](https://github.com/kzarzycki/dotagents/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/loop.md))
- **loop**: Iteratively identifies, implements, and verifies LongLink improvements after the user selects them. ([Sau1707/openconfig](https://github.com/Sau1707/openconfig/blob/HEAD/agents/loop.md))
- **loop**: ? ([shigurenimo/claude-plugins](https://github.com/shigurenimo/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/product/agents/loop.md))
- **LOOP**: Run shape: `while :; do cat agents/LOOP.md | claude ; done`, or `GOVFOLIO_LANES=N ./agents/run-loop.sh` — lane 0 runs this protocol; lanes 1..N-1 run agents/PROMPT-FACTORY-LANE.md per agents/workflows/factory-lane.md (goal 097). ([ssmleo/govfolio](https://github.com/ssmleo/govfolio/blob/HEAD/agents/LOOP.md))
- **Loop Orchestrator**: Guides features through Blueprint, UI Tokens, Code Inspection, Checkpoint, and Triage. ([iging/sauron](https://github.com/iging/sauron/blob/HEAD/core/agents/loop-orchestrator.md) · ★8)
- **Loop Orchestrator**: Orchestreert een begrensde planner-implementer-reviewer-loop voor een goedgekeurde feature. ([DIKW/ai-ondersteund-ontwikkelen](https://github.com/DIKW/ai-ondersteund-ontwikkelen/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/loop-orchestrator.agent.md) · ★1)
- **loop-ac**: Resume-Tailor development-loop AC seat (Opus). Writes the acceptance criteria that bind every downstream seat - design, plan, tests and implementation all build to them. Elevated above loop-seat because a criterion that cannot fail, or that demands the wrong thing, is inherited by the whole chunk. Use only when a development-loop brief names the AC seat. ([alexandergshaw/Resume-Tailor](https://github.com/alexandergshaw/Resume-Tailor/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/loop-ac.md))
- **loop-ac**: The acceptance-criteria seat - the pass that turns the owner's words into the criteria every later seat is measured against. Use for the criteria round on any backlog item. Not for mechanism, not for oracle construction, not for production code, and never for checking an artifact it authored. ([alexandergshaw/teaching-assistant](https://github.com/alexandergshaw/teaching-assistant/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/loop-ac.md))
- **loop-adversary**: Read-only adversary for positive committed-record experiment results. ([boseranit/agent-control-plane](https://github.com/boseranit/agent-control-plane/blob/HEAD/agent_control_plane/research_loop/agents/loop-adversary.md))
- **loop-advisor**: Read-only advisor for the work-on-task-loop skill. Gets a loop that stopped making progress, finds the cause of the dead end from the loop state folder, and returns a strategy or the questions that the user must answer. ([wojd0/dotfiles](https://github.com/wojd0/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/loop-advisor.md))
- **loop-agent**: Execute workflow agents iteratively for refinement and progressive improvement. Invoke when you need repetitive refinement, multi-iteration tasks (code review loops, incremental improvements), progressive optimization, or feedback loops until quality criteria are met or max iterations reached. ([d-o-hub/rust-self-learning-memory](https://github.com/d-o-hub/rust-self-learning-memory/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/loop-agent.md) · ★14 · 5 copies)
- **loop-agent**: Executes one iteration of a loop — performs the task and judges whether the loop's objective is met from the actual repo state. Spawned as a background agent by the loop skill; one agent per iteration, no separate judge. ([cloudvoyant/codevoyant](https://github.com/cloudvoyant/codevoyant/blob/HEAD/skills/loop/agents/loop-agent.md))
- **loop-alert-root-cause**: Finds the root cause of one open alert in the inbox. Reads the alert, its item, its runs and the open faults, and returns one finding. Never answers the alert and never moves its item. The coordinator dispatches one run for each open alert that holds no finding, and it records the finding on the alert. ([iantanwx/factory](https://github.com/iantanwx/factory/blob/HEAD/pack/agents/loop-alert-root-cause.md))
- **loop-analyst**: Read-only: drafts one proposal per finding that has a lever from an autonomous-dev-loop findings export (used by /loop-insights). ([koydas/ai-dev-tools](https://github.com/koydas/ai-dev-tools/blob/HEAD/agents/loop-analyst.md) · ★1)
- **loop-approver**: Decides whether a build loop run merges, checks it after the merge, and hands back to the owner with concerns when it doesn't hold. ([kylegani/build-loop](https://github.com/kylegani/build-loop/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/loop-approver.md))
- **loop-architect**: A specialist agent for shaping autoresearch-style and standing-runtime feedback loops into explicit Infinite Brain artifacts without inventing a new entity. ([starmynd-org/infinite-brain-os](https://github.com/starmynd-org/infinite-brain-os/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/loop-architect.md) · ★301 · 3 copies)
- **loop-architect**: Interactive designer for agentic loops in Claude Code. Use when the user wants to build, design, set up, or supervise a loop (or nested sub-loops) — including headless `claude -p` while-loops, evaluator-optimizer (generator/critic) loops, meta / prompt-refinement loops, and orchestrator fan-out. Interrogates the goal, forces it to be binary and verifiable, picks the right pattern, scaffolds the concrete files, and wires in guardrails (exit condition, max-iteration cap, in-code budget enforcement, sandbox, human checkpoint). ([fltman/loop-engineer](https://github.com/fltman/loop-engineer/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/loop-architect.md) · ★38)
- **loop-architect**: Єдиний, хто будує, покращує і СЕРТИФІКУЄ петлі DeliveryOS. Виклич на build нової / improve наявної / verify петлі. Застосовує рубрику M1–M11 + anti-cheat dry-run; не випускає несертифіковану петлю. Не диспатчить і не запускає петлі в проді (це Orchestrator). ([SyniakSviatoslav/dowiz](https://github.com/SyniakSviatoslav/dowiz/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/loop-architect.md) · ★2)
- **loop-architect**: Read-only loop/harness architect grounded in the control plane. Diagnoses a target project's ETCLOVG coverage and returns a scored report with a maturity level. Never modifies files. ([hhamja/claude-code-harness](https://github.com/hhamja/claude-code-harness/blob/HEAD/agents/loop-architect.md) · ★1)
- **loop-architect**: Architekt im allgemeinen Agentic-Loop-Kern. Leitet aus Spec, Feature-Liste, Prüfungen und vorhandenem Code eine Aufteilung in kleine Einheiten mit Verträgen ab und ordnet jedem Feature die Einheiten zu, die es berührt, und die Features, von denen es abhängt. Grundlage für paralleles Bauen und gezieltes Prüfen. Ändert nie Code. ([ueberproduct-lab/aristo-m64](https://github.com/ueberproduct-lab/aristo-m64/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/loop-architect.md) · ★1)
- **loop-architect**: Resume-Tailor development-loop STRUCTURE seat (Opus). Produces the 1b structural design - module and table boundaries, seams, contracts, data flow, migrations - or its 8b/8c follow-up. Elevated above loop-seat because a wrong contract is inherited by the plan, the tests and every implementer round after it. Use only when a development-loop brief names the structure seat. ([alexandergshaw/Resume-Tailor](https://github.com/alexandergshaw/Resume-Tailor/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/loop-architect.md))
- **loop-architect**: The architecture and design seat - the pass that decides SHAPE. Use for the architect pass, the seam or contract design inside a scoped item, and any design artifact whose choices every later wave is built against. Not for writing production code, not for test notes (use loop-test-author), and never for checking an artifact it authored. ([alexandergshaw/teaching-assistant](https://github.com/alexandergshaw/teaching-assistant/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/loop-architect.md))
- **loop-architect**: &gt; Designs the referral loop's mechanics + the K-factor and cycle-time math — the structure that determines whether the loop compounds. Computes K from labeled inputs; never asserts it. ([forsvn-labs/conquistador](https://github.com/forsvn-labs/conquistador/blob/HEAD/skills/conquistador/plays/referral/agents/loop-architect.md))
- **loop-architect**: Resolves an escalated design decision. Read-only. ([rbross-hpc/pub-analysis](https://github.com/rbross-hpc/pub-analysis/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/loop-architect.md))
- **loop-architect-agent**: You define the operating program for one measurable loop. ([forsvn-labs/conquistador](https://github.com/forsvn-labs/conquistador/blob/HEAD/skills/conquistador/plays/experiment/agents/loop-architect-agent.md))
- **loop-assist**: Loop iteration enforcement agent. Certifies GO/NO-GO between iterations of a long-running task: green regression baseline, coherent loop state, fresh handoff, clean tree, and testable acceptance criteria for the next milestone. Repairs loop-state artifacts only; returns code deficiencies as a fix-list for the Developer agent. ([aj-geddes/unicorn-team](https://github.com/aj-geddes/unicorn-team/blob/HEAD/agents/loop-assist.md) · ★14)
- **loop-auditor**: Adversarial reviewer. Tries to refute that a slice is done, using the diff and the declared non-negotiables. Read-only. ([cbdreamer11/CB-loop-kit-claude-plugin](https://github.com/cbdreamer11/CB-loop-kit-claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/loop-auditor.md) · ★8)
- **loop-auditor**: Nemesis, The Reckoner (auditor). Reviewer of the reviewers. After a goal closes or holds, audits the leader, scout, writer and reviewer from their artefacts alone (plan.md, journal, evidence, report.md, git log), never from their reasoning, and returns one AUDIT block per role. Read-only. Dispatched by the orchestrator or started by the owner with claude --agent loop-auditor. ([camilopolo1991-wq/The-Forge](https://github.com/camilopolo1991-wq/The-Forge/blob/HEAD/agents/loop-auditor.md))
- **loop-auditor**: Audits agent definitions (currently .energy format) for completeness, token efficiency, and harness quality. ([naman10parikh/acorn](https://github.com/naman10parikh/acorn/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/loop-auditor.md) · 17 copies)
- **loop-auditor**: Audits one project implementation step ([rbross-hpc/pub-analysis](https://github.com/rbross-hpc/pub-analysis/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/loop-auditor.md))
- **loop-autonomous**: `loop-runbook.md`（1イテレーションの手順）を**自分のペースで連続実行**するための上位ドライバ。 夜間にこれを `/loop` に渡して起動し、総監督が `Approved`(GO) にした Issue を空になるまで処理して、 あとはアイドル（GO 待ち）でスリープし続ける。**GO が無い夜は一切トークンを使わない**のが要。 ([ip-san/fde-agile-quest](https://github.com/ip-san/fde-agile-quest/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/loop-autonomous.md))
- **loop-autonomous-playtest**: `loop-autonomous.md`（GO ゲート版）の**完全自走バージョン**。人間の GO（`Approved` 承認）を外し、 **playtest が仕事を自分で発見してボードに積み、エンジンが実装して PR まで出す**——夜が回るほど ゲームが自律的に磨かれる自己給餌ループ。 ([ip-san/fde-agile-quest](https://github.com/ip-san/fde-agile-quest/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/loop-autonomous-playtest.md))
- **loop-board-amplifier**: Loop Board amplifier (creative track). Turns a thin creative brief into an ambitious spec + a gradeable ambition rubric, harvesting best-in-class references from the web and inventorying reusable tools/skills/MCPs. Runs on a strong model. ([mabualsoud/loop-board](https://github.com/mabualsoud/loop-board/blob/HEAD/agents/loop-board-amplifier.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **loop-board-auditor**: Loop Board auditor. Analyzes a problem through ONE assigned lens (correctness, security, completeness, simplicity, regression, or operability) and returns a distilled plan with evidence. Spawn 2-N in parallel, each with a different lens. ([mabualsoud/loop-board](https://github.com/mabualsoud/loop-board/blob/HEAD/agents/loop-board-auditor.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **loop-board-builder**: Loop Board builder (creative track). Owns the ENTIRE artifact and builds/polishes it against an ambitious spec + ambition rubric, reusing the capability plan (design MCP, domain skills, harvested patterns) instead of reinventing. Never fragments a holistic artifact. ([mabualsoud/loop-board](https://github.com/mabualsoud/loop-board/blob/HEAD/agents/loop-board-builder.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **loop-board-critic**: Loop Board adversarial critic. Refutation-first reviewer that assumes a solved item is actually wrong and hunts for blockers (missing cases, false assumptions, requirements not met, checks that pass for the wrong reason). Read-only — never edits. Run N times for a majority-vote verdict on high-stakes items. ([mabualsoud/loop-board](https://github.com/mabualsoud/loop-board/blob/HEAD/agents/loop-board-critic.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **loop-board-implementer**: Loop Board implementer. Implements ONE board item exactly per the frozen plan, touching only the files in scope, never editing or weakening the verification check, and leaving a clean committed state. ([mabualsoud/loop-board](https://github.com/mabualsoud/loop-board/blob/HEAD/agents/loop-board-implementer.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **loop-board-judge**: Loop Board judge (creative track). Independently scores a finished creative artifact against the ambition rubric, verifying every claimed feature against the actual artifact (never the self-report). Spawn N (default 3) for a trusted averaged grade. ([mabualsoud/loop-board](https://github.com/mabualsoud/loop-board/blob/HEAD/agents/loop-board-judge.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **loop-board-playtester**: Loop Board playtester & ambition critic (creative track). LIVE-tests the artifact (runs/renders/drives it, screenshots key states) then critiques for AMBITION — required to push "go further", naming the weakest rubric dimensions with specific upgrades. The opposite of the correctness-only adversarial critic. ([mabualsoud/loop-board](https://github.com/mabualsoud/loop-board/blob/HEAD/agents/loop-board-playtester.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **loop-board-reconciler**: Loop Board reconciler. Takes N independent auditor plans and merges them into ONE plan in a single pass, resolving each disagreement with a reason grounded in the requirements, and listing any conflict it cannot resolve for escalation. ([mabualsoud/loop-board](https://github.com/mabualsoud/loop-board/blob/HEAD/agents/loop-board-reconciler.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **loop-board-reporter**: Loop Board reporter and rubric judge. In a single call, scores the final solution analytically (0.0-1.0 per criterion + overall PASS/FAIL) and writes the board report with per-item status, residual risks, and escalations. ([mabualsoud/loop-board](https://github.com/mabualsoud/loop-board/blob/HEAD/agents/loop-board-reporter.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **loop-board-researcher**: Loop Board researcher. Answers one focused sub-question to unblock the board, searching wide then narrow, preferring primary sources, and returning a distilled cited summary. Runs on a cheap model. ([mabualsoud/loop-board](https://github.com/mabualsoud/loop-board/blob/HEAD/agents/loop-board-researcher.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **loop-board-verifier**: Loop Board verifier. Blackbox-tests a completed item against ALL success criteria, running the complete check set plus at least one negative case, and grades PASS only if every criterion is MET with real evidence. ([mabualsoud/loop-board](https://github.com/mabualsoud/loop-board/blob/HEAD/agents/loop-board-verifier.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **loop-breaker**: Cold-eyes analysis worker for forqsite.help. Loads the loop-breaker procedure skill and proposes one alternative approach after a builder has failed twice on the same error. ([kewlwerks/forqsite.help](https://github.com/kewlwerks/forqsite.help/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/loop-breaker.md))
- **loop-briefer**: Writes and revises production briefs for Soundlines genre loops (docs/loops/briefs/&lt;slug&gt;.json): genre-authentic tempo, meter, key, bars, seeds and an ACE-Step prompt with loop-form, mix and originality clauses, passing check-brief.mjs. Use to plan a loop for a new genre or to apply loop-review feedback (vocals, intro, breakdown, muffled mix, wrong groove). Does not render audio. ([alexandrehebert/soundlines](https://github.com/alexandrehebert/soundlines/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/loop-briefer.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **loop-budget**: Keep an agent loop inside its declared budget and end it with a typed verdict instead of a runaway process. Use when a repair, polling, or fan-out loop must declare step/cost/wall-time budgets up f... ([quantumquirkxyz/skills-quirk](https://github.com/quantumquirkxyz/skills-quirk/blob/HEAD/.agents/skills/platform/adapters/claude/agents/delivery-loop-budget.md))
- **loop-budget-guard**: 장기 루프 작업 중 예산/한도 점검을 요청받을 때 위임 — 누적 토큰·비용·반복·시간이 BUDGET/STOP 상한을 넘었는지 판정하고 중단 신호를 반환. ([successbank/ultrav](https://github.com/successbank/ultrav/blob/HEAD/V9/%EA%B0%9C%EB%B0%9C%ED%8C%80%20V9/%EA%B0%9C%EB%B0%9C%ED%8C%80%20%EC%97%90%EC%9D%B4%EC%A0%84%ED%8A%B8%20V9/dot-claude/agents/loop-budget-guard.md))
- **loop-budget-guard**: BUDGET/STOP을 결정적으로 집행. 누적 토큰·비용·반복·시간 상한 초과 시 루프 중단 신호. 매 tick 경계에서 트리거. ([successbank/ultrav](https://github.com/successbank/ultrav/blob/HEAD/V9/%EA%B0%9C%EB%B0%9C%ED%8C%80%20V9/%EA%B0%9C%EB%B0%9C%ED%8C%80%20%EC%97%90%EC%9D%B4%EC%A0%84%ED%8A%B8%20V9/dot-codex/agents/loop-budget-guard.md))
- **loop-builder**: Implements one slice completely inside an assigned file tree. Reads real code before writing. Never publishes. ([cbdreamer11/CB-loop-kit-claude-plugin](https://github.com/cbdreamer11/CB-loop-kit-claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/loop-builder.md) · ★8)
- **loop-builder**: Opus builder for the loop-issues cycle — one phase per spawn (`contract`, `build &lt;k&gt;` or `ship &lt;r&gt;`), medium effort, fresh context each time. One issue, one worktree. ([rbaumier/skills](https://github.com/rbaumier/skills/blob/HEAD/loop-issues/agents/loop-builder.md) · ★6)
- **loop-builder**: Implements tasks and fixes failures reported by loop-checker. Build and fix only — never judges completion. ([sdsrss/loop_eng](https://github.com/sdsrss/loop_eng/blob/HEAD/agents/loop-builder.md) · ★3)
- **loop-builder**: Baut genau ein Feature eines Projekts gegen dessen Spec und das eingefrorene Eval (allgemeiner Agentic-Loop-Kern). Ändert nur die in loop.config.json als beschreibbar erklärten Pfade. Wird von der Schleife .claude/workflows/agentic-loop.js eingesetzt. ([ueberproduct-lab/aristo-m64](https://github.com/ueberproduct-lab/aristo-m64/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/loop-builder.md) · ★1)
- **loop-builder**: Builder agent that implements code changes for a specific feature sequentially. ([jakemaly/pi](https://github.com/jakemaly/pi/blob/HEAD/agent/agents/loop-builder.md))
- **loop-builder**: Builds one task of the build loop in the files it owns, without starting servers, browsers, builds or test runs. ([kylegani/build-loop](https://github.com/kylegani/build-loop/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/loop-builder.md))
- **loop-builder**: Implements one bounded project task. ([rbross-hpc/pub-analysis](https://github.com/rbross-hpc/pub-analysis/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/loop-builder.md))
- **loop-checker**: Independently scores a loop iteration's output against a rubric and returns a structured verdict. Adversarial by design: finds faults, does not confirm. Used by auto-research and future Maker-Checker loops. ([kbanc85/claudia](https://github.com/kbanc85/claudia/blob/HEAD/template-v2/.claude/agents/loop-checker.md) · ★301 · 2 copies)
- **loop-checker**: Runs the round's checks — the contract's criteria, plus the full project sweep when the round is not scoped to one backlog item — and reports failures with file:line precision. Called after loop-builder. Never modifies code. ([sdsrss/loop_eng](https://github.com/sdsrss/loop_eng/blob/HEAD/agents/loop-checker.md) · ★3)
- **loop-checker**: Resume-Tailor development-loop FRESH adversarial checker (Opus). Tries to break one artifact or one group of artifacts another agent produced - chunking and triage, AC, the G3 operate/attack/break group, reuse survey, plan (and its 3b re-triage), 3b facts, verification findings and trigger rulings, unit-test notes, accessibility findings, an adjudication, an RCA, a regression result's agent-executed buckets. Never checks its own work and never edits the artifact. Use only when a development-loop brief names the check. ([alexandergshaw/Resume-Tailor](https://github.com/alexandergshaw/Resume-Tailor/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/loop-checker.md))
- **loop-checker**: The adversarial check over one model-authored artifact, run before its consumer reads it. Use after any seat produces acceptance criteria, a design pass, a plan, test notes, an RCA, or a chunking - and to check the orchestrator's own rulings. Never use on an artifact this agent authored. ([alexandergshaw/teaching-assistant](https://github.com/alexandergshaw/teaching-assistant/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/loop-checker.md))
- **loop-checker**: ループの判定専用エージェント(Checker)。Maker の変更を fresh context で独立検証する。 修正は物理的に行えない(Write/Edit なし)。検証ゲートの再実行・仕様との突き合わせ・ 報告スキーマ(High/Medium/Low + 根拠)での判定のみを行う。ループ1イテレーションの 検証フェーズで使う。 ([comoc/loop-engineering-setup](https://github.com/comoc/loop-engineering-setup/blob/HEAD/sample-repo/.claude/agents/loop-checker.md))
- **loop-checkout-forensics**: Finds out why one run's checkout still stands, or what deleted it. Reads the run record, the item and the checkout itself, and deletes nothing. Returns the state of the checkout and the rule that decided. The coordinator dispatches one run for each checkout it asks about. ([iantanwx/factory](https://github.com/iantanwx/factory/blob/HEAD/pack/agents/loop-checkout-forensics.md))
- **loop-cleanup**: The maintainer in the /albert harness. Keeps the working tree merge-ready between iterations, dead code, lint/format, dupes, tidy, without changing behavior. Runs every N iterations and before any terminal stop. Spawned by the Albert controller. ([Sdraugel/albert](https://github.com/Sdraugel/albert/blob/HEAD/harness/agents/loop-cleanup.md) · ★108)
- **loop-coder**: Implementation agent for dispatched task contracts. Briefed per-task by the dispatch foreman with a freshly written prompt; this file is briefing doctrine, not a fill-in template. ([JNK234/claude-kit](https://github.com/JNK234/claude-kit/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev-tools/agents/loop-coder.md))
- **loop-cold-reader**: Zero-context storyboard evaluator for Loop Studio showcase animations. Given ONLY a path to storyboard.png, inspects the image and reports in one plain sentence what the depicted project or tool does, plus any unreadable or confusing panels. Never reads README.md, showcase.json, or source code. ([MarcusK00/loop-studio](https://github.com/MarcusK00/loop-studio/blob/HEAD/agents/loop-cold-reader.md) · ★1)
